#### [Gas Royalty]{.HTitleText2} {#gas-royalty .SchedHeadL2}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of royalty --- gas

  - **4** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When gas that is recovered from, or
    attributed to, lands in a contract area is sold, the royalty payable
    is the gross royalty value of the gas, determined in accordance with
    subsection (2), less the portion of the cost of gathering,
    dehydrating, compressing and processing the gas that is equal to its
    gross royalty value divided by its total value.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Gross royalty

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} The gross royalty value of gas that is recovered
    from, or attributed to, lands in the contract area is the basic
    gross royalty value of 25% of the quantity of that gas multiplied by
    the actual selling price plus the supplementary gross royalty value
    determined in accordance with subsection (3). All amounts are to be
    calculated at the time and place of production.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supplementary gross royalty

    [(3)]{.lawlabel} The supplementary gross royalty value of gas,
    individually determined for each gas component produced, is equal to
    the sum of the products obtained by multiplying 75% of the quantity
    of each gas component by

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of marketable gas,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the actual selling price exceeds
        \$10.65/1000 m^3^ but does not exceed \$24.85/1000 m^3^, 30% of
        the difference between the actual selling price per 1000 m^3^
        and \$10.65/1000 m^3^, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the actual selling price exceeds
        \$24.85/1000 m^3^, \$4.26/1000 m^3^ plus 55% of the portion of
        the actual selling price in excess of \$24.85/1000 m^3^;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of pentanes plus, if the actual
      selling price exceeds \$27.68/m^3^, 50% of the portion of the
      actual selling price in excess of \$27.68/m^3^;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of sulphur, if the actual selling
      price exceeds \$39.37/t, 50% of the portion of the actual selling
      price in excess of \$39.37/t;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of other components from a source
      that produces marketable gas, an amount equal to the product
      obtained by multiplying the actual selling price of each of those
      components by the percentage by which the overall royalty rate for
      marketable gas, taking both basic and supplementary gross royalty
      values into account, exceeds 25%; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of other components from a source
      that does not produce marketable gas, the lesser of one third of
      the actual selling price of that component and the amount
      determined under any special agreement entered into under
      subsection 4(2) of the Act.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measurement of volumes

    [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, volumes referred
    to are volumes measured at standard conditions of 101.325 kPa and
    15°C.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to council

    [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must send the council notice of any
    costs that are deducted under subsection (1) for gathering,
    dehydrating, compressing and processing.

#### [Royalty on Oil or Gas Consumed]{.HTitleText2} {#royalty-on-oil-or-gas-consumed .SchedHeadL2}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No royalty payable

  - **5** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 2 to 4, the royalty payable
    on oil or gas recovered from, or attributed to, lands in a contract
    area is nil if the oil or gas is consumed in drilling for, producing
    or processing oil or gas that is recovered from, or attributed to,
    those lands.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Royalty payable

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, subsection (1) does not apply to oil or
    gas that is consumed in the production or processing of crude
    bitumen.

::::: section
:::: {#1141288 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 6]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 113)]{.OriginatingRef}[Administrative Monetary Penalties]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1141289 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="1141290">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top alignleft" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
